   "source": [
    "real_weights = {}\n",
    "\n",
    "for experiment in experiments:\n",
    "    experiment_name = \"v\".join(e.upper() for e in experiment)\n",
    "\n",
    "    for te_type in ['L', 'S']:\n",
    "        run_key = f\"{experiment_name}@{te_type}TE\"\n",
    "        run_data = run_summary_df[run_key]\n",
    "\n",
    "        weights_matrix = run_data['weights']\n",
    "        labels = run_data['labels']\n",
    "\n",
    "        # Build a DataFrame where each column is a weight (w1, w2, ...) and a true_label\n",
    "        weight_rows = []\n",
    "        for weight_vector, label in zip(weights_matrix, labels):\n",
    "            row = {'true_label': label}\n",
    "            # Assign weights dynamically: w1, w2, w3, ...\n",
    "            for i, w in enumerate(weight_vector, start=1):\n",
    "                row[f'w{i}'] = w\n",
    "            weight_rows.append(row)\n",
    "\n",
    "        # Store as DataFrame\n",
    "        real_weights[run_key] = pd.DataFrame(weight_rows)\n",
    "\n",
    "if debug: print(real_weights)"
   ]

   "execution_count": null,
   "id": "0d0f7807-d074-4d20-9aa2-c159e2effee6",
   "metadata": {},
   "outputs": [],
   "source": [
    "import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n",
    "from mpl_toolkits.mplot3d import Axes3D\n",
    "import seaborn as sns\n",
    "import numpy as np\n",
    "import pandas as pd\n",
    "from matplotlib.colors import to_rgb, rgb_to_hsv, hsv_to_rgb\n",
    "\n",
    "def shade_by_distance(base_color, distance, dmin, dmax):\n",
    "    \"\"\"\n",
    "    Produce clearly distinguishable light/dark shades without blowing out colour.\n",
    "    \"\"\"\n",
    "    # normalise distance to [0, 1]\n",
    "    if dmax == dmin:\n",
    "        t = 0.5\n",
    "    else:\n",
    "        t = (distance - dmin) / (dmax - dmin)\n",
    "\n",
    "    # Convert to HSV\n",
    "    r, g, b = to_rgb(base_color)\n",
    "    h, s, v = rgb_to_hsv([r, g, b])\n",
    "\n",
    "    # -------------------------------\n",
    "    # Controlled brightness range\n",
    "    # -------------------------------\n",
    "    v_dark  = 0.10\n",
    "    v_light = 0.95\n",
    "    v_new   = v_dark + (v_light - v_dark) * (1 - t)  # closer → lighter\n",
    "\n",
    "    # -------------------------------\n",
    "    # Small saturation correction\n",
    "    # -------------------------------\n",
    "    # Further points (dark) get slightly more saturation (pops more)\n",
    "    # Closer points (light) get slightly less saturation\n",
    "    s_min = max(0, s - 0.15)\n",
    "    s_max = min(1, s + 0.10)\n",
    "    s_new = s_max - (s_max - s_min) * (1 - t)\n",
    "\n",
    "    # Convert back\n",
    "    r2, g2, b2 = hsv_to_rgb([h, s_new, v_new])\n",
    "    return (r2, g2, b2)\n",
    "\n",
    "\n",
    "def plot_real_weights_and_spectra(run_key, reconstructed_data, run_metadata, colours_dict_label=None):\n",
    "    \"\"\"\n",
    "    Plot real cNMF weights + reconstructed spectra with brightness varying\n",
    "    by distance from origin.\n",
    "    \"\"\"\n",
    "\n",
    "    # -----------------------\n",
    "    #  Extract data\n",
    "    # -----------------------\n",
    "    df_recon = reconstructed_data[run_key].copy()\n",
    "    labels = df_recon['labels'].copy()\n",
    "    df_recon = df_recon.drop(columns=['labels'])\n",
    "\n",
    "    weights = pd.DataFrame(run_metadata[run_key][\"weights\"]).copy()\n",
    "    n_sources = weights.shape[1]\n",
    "    weights.columns = [f\"w{i+1}\" for i in range(n_sources)]\n",
    "    weights[\"label\"] = labels.values\n",
    "\n",
    "    # base label colours\n",
    "    unique_labels = sorted(weights['label'].unique())\n",
    "    label_to_colour = {lab: colours_dict_label.get(lab, \"black\") for lab in unique_labels}\n",
    "\n",
    "    # -----------------------\n",
    "    #  Distance → brightness\n",
    "    # -----------------------\n",
    "    w_vals = weights[[f\"w{i+1}\" for i in range(n_sources)]].values\n",
    "    distances = np.linalg.norm(w_vals, axis=1)   # Euclidean distance from origin\n",
    "    dmin, dmax = distances.min(), distances.max()\n",
    "\n",
    "    # Create per-point colours\n",
    "    per_point_colours = []\n",
    "    for idx, row in weights.iterrows():\n",
    "        base = label_to_colour[row[\"label\"]]\n",
    "        dist = distances[idx]\n",
    "        shade = shade_by_distance(base, dist, dmin, dmax)\n",
    "        per_point_colours.append(shade)\n",
    "\n",
    "    weights[\"shade\"] = per_point_colours\n",
    "\n",
    "    # -----------------------\n",
    "    #  Figure layout\n",
    "    # -----------------------\n",
    "    fig = plt.figure(figsize=(6 * (1 + len(unique_labels)), 6))\n",
    "\n",
    "    # 3D scatter if >=3 sources\n",
    "    if n_sources >= 3:\n",
    "        ax0 = fig.add_subplot(1, 1 + len(unique_labels), 1, projection='3d')\n",
    "        for lab in unique_labels:\n",
    "            subset = weights[weights[\"label\"] == lab]\n",
    "            ax0.scatter(\n",
    "                subset[\"w1\"], subset[\"w2\"], subset[\"w3\"],\n",
    "                color=list(subset[\"shade\"].values),\n",
    "                label=lab\n",
    "            )\n",
    "        ax0.set_zlabel(\"w3\")\n",
    "\n",
    "    else:\n",
    "        ax0 = fig.add_subplot(1, 1 + len(unique_labels), 1)\n",
    "        sns.scatterplot(\n",
    "            data=weights,\n",
    "            x=\"w1\", y=\"w2\",\n",
    "            hue=\"label\",  # still group by label for legend\n",
    "            palette=label_to_colour,\n",
    "            ax=ax0,\n",
    "            legend=False  # we handle legend manually\n",
    "        )\n",
    "        # overlay shaded points\n",
    "        ax0.scatter(\n",
    "            weights[\"w1\"], weights[\"w2\"],\n",
    "            c=list(weights[\"shade\"].values)\n",
    "        )\n",
    "\n",
    "    ax0.set_title(f\"Weights – {run_key}\")\n",
    "    ax0.set_xlabel(\"w1\")\n",
    "    ax0.set_ylabel(\"w2\")\n",
    "    ax0.legend()\n",
    "    ax0.grid(True)\n",
    "\n",
    "    # -----------------------\n",
    "    #  Plot reconstructed spectra per label, using shaded colour\n",
    "    # -----------------------\n",
    "    for i, lab in enumerate(unique_labels):\n",
    "        ax = fig.add_subplot(1, 1 + len(unique_labels), i + 2)\n",
    "\n",
    "        subset_idx = np.where(labels == lab)[0]\n",
    "        shades_subset = weights.loc[weights[\"label\"] == lab, \"shade\"].values\n",
    "\n",
    "        for j, col in zip(subset_idx, shades_subset):\n",
    "            ax.plot(df_recon.iloc[j], color=col, alpha=0.9)\n",
    "\n",
    "        ax.set_title(f\"{lab} Spectra\")\n",
    "        ax.set_xlabel(\"Index (or ppm axis)\")\n",
    "        ax.set_ylabel(\"Intensity\")\n",
    "        ax.grid(True)\n",
    "\n",
    "    fig.suptitle(f\"Real Data – {run_key}\", fontsize=16)\n",
    "    plt.tight_layout()\n",
    "    #plt.show()\n",
    "    plt.close(fig)\n",
    "\n"
   ]
  },

   "execution_count": 28,
   "id": "44430cce-e05f-413f-a1b7-e169d54a3be5",

   "source": [
    "import numpy as np\n",
    "import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n",
    "from scipy.stats import zscore\n",
    "from matplotlib import lines as mlines, colors as mcolors\n",
    "from numpy.linalg import eigh\n",
    "from mpl_toolkits.mplot3d.art3d import Poly3DCollection\n",
    "\n",
    "# ---------------------------------------------------------\n",
    "# Compute global axes limits\n",
    "# ---------------------------------------------------------\n",
    "def compute_global_axes_limits(real_weights):\n",
    "    x_min, x_max = np.inf, -np.inf\n",
    "    y_min, y_max = np.inf, -np.inf\n",
    "    z_min, z_max = np.inf, -np.inf\n",
    "    \n",
    "    for df in real_weights.values():\n",
    "        if \"w1\" in df: x_min = min(x_min, df[\"w1\"].min()); x_max = max(x_max, df[\"w1\"].max())\n",
    "        if \"w2\" in df: y_min = min(y_min, df[\"w2\"].min()); y_max = max(y_max, df[\"w2\"].max())\n",
    "        if \"w3\" in df: z_min = min(z_min, df[\"w3\"].min()); z_max = max(z_max, df[\"w3\"].max())\n",
    "\n",
    "    x_buffer = (x_max - x_min) * 0.05 if x_max > x_min else 0.1\n",
    "    y_buffer = (y_max - y_min) * 0.05 if y_max > y_min else 0.1\n",
    "    z_buffer = (z_max - z_min) * 0.05 if z_max > z_min else 0.1\n",
    "\n",
    "    xlims = (x_min - x_buffer, x_max + x_buffer)\n",
    "    ylims = (y_min - y_buffer, y_max + y_buffer)\n",
    "    zlims = (z_min - z_buffer, z_max + z_buffer) if z_min < np.inf else None\n",
    "\n",
    "    return xlims, ylims, zlims\n",
    "\n",
    "\n",
    "# ---------------------------------------------------------\n",
    "# 3D Gaussian ellipsoid (analytic, stable)\n",
    "# ---------------------------------------------------------\n",
    "def plot_gaussian_ellipsoid(ax, mean, cov, n_std=1.0, color=\"C0\", alpha=0.15, n_points=40):\n",
    "    vals, vecs = eigh(cov)\n",
    "    radii = n_std * np.sqrt(np.maximum(vals, 0))\n",
    "\n",
    "    u = np.linspace(0, 2*np.pi, n_points)\n",
    "    v = np.linspace(0, np.pi, n_points)\n",
    "\n",
    "    x = np.outer(np.cos(u), np.sin(v))\n",
    "    y = np.outer(np.sin(u), np.sin(v))\n",
    "    z = np.outer(np.ones_like(u), np.cos(v))\n",
    "\n",
    "    xyz = np.stack((x, y, z), axis=-1)\n",
    "    xyz *= radii\n",
    "    xyz = xyz @ vecs.T\n",
    "    xyz += mean\n",
    "\n",
    "    X, Y, Z = xyz[...,0], xyz[...,1], xyz[...,2]\n",
    "    ax.plot_surface(X, Y, Z, rstride=1, cstride=1,\n",
    "                    color=color, alpha=alpha,\n",
    "                    linewidth=0, shade=True)\n",
    "\n",
    "\n",
    "# ---------------------------------------------------------\n",
    "# MAIN PLOTTING FUNCTION (2D + 3D)\n",
    "# ---------------------------------------------------------\n",
    "def plot_weights_with_kde_and_outliers(real_weights, colours_dict_label, outlier_sd=3.0, gridsize=60):\n",
    "\n",
    "    xlims, ylims, zlims = compute_global_axes_limits(real_weights)\n",
    "\n",
    "    for key, df in real_weights.items():\n",
    "        df = df.copy()\n",
    "        print(\"Plotting\", key)\n",
    "\n",
    "        if \"true_label\" not in df and \"label\" in df:\n",
    "            df[\"true_label\"] = df[\"label\"]\n",
    "\n",
    "        # -----------------------------------------------------\n",
    "        # 2D PLOT\n",
    "        # -----------------------------------------------------\n",
    "        if set([\"w1\",\"w2\"]).issubset(df.columns) and \"w3\" not in df.columns:\n",
    "            fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(10,10), dpi=500)\n",
    "            all_out = []\n",
    "            unique_labels = sorted(df[\"true_label\"].unique())\n",
    "\n",
    "            for label in unique_labels:\n",
    "                sub = df[df[\"true_label\"] == label].copy()\n",
    "                if sub.empty: continue\n",
    "\n",
    "                # z-score outlier detection\n",
    "                zs = np.abs(sub[[\"w1\",\"w2\"]].apply(zscore))\n",
    "                out_mask = (zs > outlier_sd).any(axis=1)\n",
    "                sub[\"is_outlier\"] = out_mask\n",
    "\n",
    "                color = colours_dict_label.get(label, \"C0\")\n",
    "                data = sub[[\"w1\",\"w2\"]].astype(float).to_numpy()\n",
    "\n",
    "                # --- Gaussian SD rings ---\n",
    "                if len(sub) > 3:\n",
    "                    mean = data.mean(axis=0)\n",
    "                    cov = np.cov(data, rowvar=False)\n",
    "                    cov += np.eye(2) * 1e-6 # THIS LINE IS SO IMPORTANT AND I DONT KNOW WHY, IF REMOVED IT CAUSES THE KERNEL PLOTTING TO BE FLAT AND CONTAIN ISLANDS\n",
    "                    xg = np.linspace(*xlims, gridsize)\n",
    "                    yg = np.linspace(*ylims, gridsize)\n",
    "                    X, Y = np.meshgrid(xg, yg)\n",
    "                    pts = np.column_stack([X.ravel(), Y.ravel()])\n",
    "\n",
    "                    diff = pts - mean\n",
    "                    cov_inv = np.linalg.pinv(cov + 1e-8*np.eye(2))\n",
    "                    d2 = np.einsum(\"ij,jk,ik->i\", diff, cov_inv, diff).reshape(X.shape)\n",
    "                    D = np.sqrt(d2)\n",
    "\n",
    "                    levels = [0,1,2,3]\n",
    "                    alphas = [0.50, 0.30, 0.15]\n",
    "\n",
    "                    for i in range(3):\n",
    "                        col = np.array(mcolors.to_rgba(color))\n",
    "                        col[3] = alphas[i]\n",
    "                        ax.contourf(X, Y, D, levels=[i, i+1], colors=[col], antialiased=True)\n",
    "\n",
    "                    ax.contour(X, Y, D, levels=[1,2,3], colors=[color], linewidths=1.2)\n",
    "\n",
    "                # --- INLIERS ---\n",
    "                ax.scatter(sub.loc[~out_mask, \"w1\"],\n",
    "                           sub.loc[~out_mask, \"w2\"],\n",
    "                           s=40, color=color, alpha=0.9, label=label, zorder=5)\n",
    "\n",
    "                # --- OUTLIERS ---\n",
    "                for _, row in sub[sub[\"is_outlier\"]].iterrows():\n",
    "                    all_out.append((label, row[\"w1\"], row[\"w2\"]))\n",
    "\n",
    "                    ax.scatter(row[\"w1\"], row[\"w2\"],\n",
    "                               s=160, facecolors=\"none\", edgecolors=\"red\",\n",
    "                               linewidths=2, zorder=3)\n",
    "                    ax.scatter(row[\"w1\"], row[\"w2\"],\n",
    "                               s=40, color=color, edgecolor=\"black\",\n",
    "                               linewidth=0.4, zorder=6)\n",
    "\n",
    "            print(\"Outliers for\", key)\n",
    "            for l,x,y in all_out:\n",
    "                print(f\"  {l}: x={x:.3f}, y={y:.3f}\")\n",
    "\n",
    "            # Legend\n",
    "            handles, labels = ax.get_legend_handles_labels()\n",
    "            uniq = {}\n",
    "            for h,l in zip(handles, labels):\n",
    "                if l not in uniq: uniq[l] = h\n",
    "            handles = list(uniq.values())\n",
    "            labels  = list(uniq.keys())\n",
    "\n",
    "            out_h = mlines.Line2D([],[], marker='o', color='none',\n",
    "                                  markerfacecolor='none', markeredgecolor='red',\n",
    "                                  markersize=10)\n",
    "            handles.append(out_h)\n",
    "            labels.append(f\"Outliers (n={len(all_out)})\")\n",
    "\n",
    "            ax.legend(handles, labels, title=\"Label\")\n",
    "            ax.set_xlim(xlims); ax.set_ylim(ylims)\n",
    "            ax.set_xlabel(\"w1\"); ax.set_ylabel(\"w2\")\n",
    "            ax.set_title(f\"2D Gaussian SD + Outliers – {key}\")\n",
    "            ax.grid(True)\n",
    "            plt.tight_layout()\n",
    "            plt.close(fig)\n",
    "            #plt.show()\n",
    "\n",
    "        # -----------------------------------------------------\n",
    "        # 3D PLOT\n",
    "        # -----------------------------------------------------\n",
    "        if set([\"w1\",\"w2\",\"w3\"]).issubset(df.columns):\n",
    "            fig = plt.figure(figsize=(10,10), dpi=500)\n",
    "            ax = fig.add_subplot(111, projection='3d')\n",
    "            all_out = []\n",
    "            unique_labels = sorted(df[\"true_label\"].unique())\n",
    "\n",
    "            for label in unique_labels:\n",
    "                sub = df[df[\"true_label\"] == label].copy()\n",
    "                if sub.empty: continue\n",
    "                color = colours_dict_label.get(label, \"C0\")\n",
    "\n",
    "                # z-score outlier detection\n",
    "                zs = np.abs(sub[[\"w1\",\"w2\",\"w3\"]].apply(zscore))\n",
    "                out_mask = (zs > outlier_sd).any(axis=1)\n",
    "                sub[\"is_outlier\"] = out_mask\n",
    "\n",
    "                # Fit Gaussian to INLIERS only\n",
    "                inliers = sub.loc[~out_mask, [\"w1\",\"w2\",\"w3\"]].astype(float).to_numpy()\n",
    "\n",
    "                if len(inliers) >= 5:\n",
    "                    mean3 = inliers.mean(axis=0)\n",
    "                    cov3  = np.cov(inliers, rowvar=False)\n",
    "\n",
    "                    # Draw ellipsoids (1σ, 2σ, 3σ)\n",
    "                    for n_std, alpha in zip([1,2,3], [0.30, 0.18, 0.10]):\n",
    "                        plot_gaussian_ellipsoid(ax, mean3, cov3,\n",
    "                                                n_std=n_std, color=color,\n",
    "                                                alpha=alpha)\n",
    "\n",
    "                # Inliers\n",
    "                ax.scatter(sub.loc[~out_mask,\"w1\"],\n",
    "                           sub.loc[~out_mask,\"w2\"],\n",
    "                           sub.loc[~out_mask,\"w3\"],\n",
    "                           s=40, color=color, alpha=0.9,\n",
    "                           label=label, zorder=5)\n",
    "\n",
    "                # Outliers\n",
    "                for _, row in sub[sub[\"is_outlier\"]].iterrows():\n",
    "                    all_out.append((label, row[\"w1\"], row[\"w2\"], row[\"w3\"]))\n",
    "                    ax.scatter(row[\"w1\"], row[\"w2\"], row[\"w3\"],\n",
    "                               s=300, facecolors=\"none\", edgecolors=\"red\",\n",
    "                               linewidths=2, zorder=3)\n",
    "                    ax.scatter(row[\"w1\"], row[\"w2\"], row[\"w3\"],\n",
    "                               s=50, color=color, edgecolor=\"black\",\n",
    "                               linewidth=0.5, zorder=6)\n",
    "\n",
    "            print(\"Outliers for\", key)\n",
    "            for l,x,y,z in all_out:\n",
    "                print(f\"  {l}: x={x:.3f}, y={y:.3f}, z={z:.3f}\")\n",
    "\n",
    "            # Legend\n",
    "            handles, labels = ax.get_legend_handles_labels()\n",
    "            uniq = {}\n",
    "            for h,l in zip(handles, labels):\n",
    "                if l not in uniq: uniq[l] = h\n",
    "            handles = list(uniq.values())\n",
    "            labels  = list(uniq.keys())\n",
    "\n",
    "            out_h = mlines.Line2D([],[], marker='o', color='none',\n",
    "                                  markerfacecolor='none', markeredgecolor='red',\n",
    "                                  markersize=10)\n",
    "            handles.append(out_h)\n",
    "            labels.append(f\"Outliers (n={len(all_out)})\")\n",
    "\n",
    "            ax.legend(handles, labels, title=\"Label\", loc='upper left')\n",
    "            ax.set_xlim(xlims); ax.set_ylim(ylims)\n",
    "            if zlims: ax.set_zlim(zlims)\n",
    "            ax.set_xlabel(\"w1\"); ax.set_ylabel(\"w2\"); ax.set_zlabel(\"w3\")\n",
    "            ax.set_title(f\"3D Gaussian Ellipsoid + Outliers – {key}\")\n",
    "            plt.tight_layout()\n",
    "            plt.close(fig)\n",
    "            #plt.show()\n"
   ]

    "def plot_raw_all(raw_data_frames, experiments):\n",
    "    for experiment in experiments:\n",
    "\n",
    "        experiment_name = 'v'.join([e.upper() for e in experiment])\n",
    "\n",
    "        fig, axs = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(14, 5), sharey=True)\n",
    "        fig.suptitle(f\"Raw spectra for {experiment_name}\", fontsize=18)\n",
    "\n",
    "        for idx, te_type in enumerate(['L', 'S']):\n",
    "            run_key = f\"{experiment_name}@{te_type}TE\"\n",
    "            \n",
    "            if run_key not in raw_data_frames:\n",
    "                print(f\"Missing: {run_key}\")\n",
    "                continue\n",
    "\n",
    "            df = raw_data_frames[run_key]\n",
    "\n",
    "            spectra_only = df.drop(columns=['labels']).values\n",
    "            labels = df['labels'].values\n",
    "\n",
    "            ax = axs[idx]\n",
    "            x = np.arange(spectra_only.shape[1])  # replace with ppm if available\n",
    "\n",
    "            for row, lab in zip(spectra_only, labels):\n",
    "                ax.plot(x, row, alpha=0.4, label=str(lab))\n",
    "\n",
    "            ax.set_title(f\"{te_type}TE (raw)\")\n",
    "            ax.set_xlabel(\"Index\")\n",
    "            ax.set_ylabel(\"Intensity\")\n",
    "            \n",
    "            # Deduplicate legend entries\n",
    "            handles, lbls = ax.get_legend_handles_labels()\n",
    "            uniq = dict(zip(lbls, handles))\n",
    "            ax.legend(uniq.values(), uniq.keys())\n",
    "\n",
    "        plt.tight_layout()\n",
    "        plt.close(fig)\n",
    "        #plt.show()\n",
    "\n",
    "\n",
    "# ---- RUN IT ----\n",
    "plot_raw_all(raw_data_frames, experiments)"
   ]
  },

    "def generate_synthetic_weights(real_data, model):\n",
    "    m_factor = 2\n",
    "    synthetic_weights = {}\n",
    "    eval_metrics = {}\n",
    "\n",
    "    for key, weights in real_data.items():\n",
    "        is2d = weights.shape[1] == 3\n",
    "        temp_synthetic_weights = []\n",
    "\n",
    "        weights_data = weights[['w1', 'w2', 'true_label']] if is2d else weights[['w1', 'w2', 'w3', 'true_label']]\n",
    "        log_l_list = []\n",
    "\n",
    "        for label in weights_data['true_label'].unique():\n",
    "            group = weights_data[weights_data['true_label'] == label][['w1', 'w2']] if is2d else \\\n",
    "                    weights_data[weights_data['true_label'] == label][['w1', 'w2', 'w3']]\n",
    "\n",
    "            group = group.values\n",
    "            try:\n",
    "                if group.shape[0] < model.n_components:\n",
    "                    continue\n",
    "            except:\n",
    "                pass\n",
    "\n",
    "            num_cases = group.shape[0]\n",
    "            model.fit(group)\n",
    "\n",
    "            # Sample until we have enough strictly positive samples\n",
    "            accepted = []\n",
    "            while len(accepted) < num_cases * m_factor:\n",
    "                needed = num_cases * m_factor - len(accepted)\n",
    "                sampled = model.sample(needed * 2)[0] if isinstance(model, GaussianMixture) else model.sample(needed * 2)\n",
    "\n",
    "                strictly_positive = sampled[(sampled > 0).all(axis=1)]\n",
    "                accepted.extend(strictly_positive[:needed])\n",
    "\n",
    "            synth_weight = np.array(accepted[:num_cases * m_factor])\n",
    "            temp_data_pt = pd.DataFrame(synth_weight, columns=['w1', 'w2'] if is2d else ['w1', 'w2', 'w3'])\n",
    "            temp_data_pt['true_label'] = label\n",
    "            temp_synthetic_weights.append(temp_data_pt)\n",
    "\n",
    "            if not isinstance(model, GaussianMixture):\n",
    "                log_l = model.score_samples(group)\n",
    "                log_l_list.extend(log_l)\n",
    "\n",
    "        synthetic_weights[key] = pd.concat(temp_synthetic_weights, ignore_index=True)\n",
    "\n",
    "        if isinstance(model, GaussianMixture):\n",
    "            eval_metrics[key] = f'{model.bic(group):.4f}'\n",
    "        else:\n",
    "            n = len(group)\n",
    "            k = group.shape[1]\n",
    "            ll = np.sum(log_l_list)\n",
    "            aic = 2 * k - 2 * ll\n",
    "            bic = np.log(n) * k - 2 * ll\n",
    "            eval_metrics[key] = f'{bic:.4f}'\n",
    "\n",
    "    return synthetic_weights, eval_metrics"
   ]
  },

    "gmm_evals = {}\n",
    "gmm_synthetic_weights = {}\n",
    "\n",
    "for n_comps in range(1, 11):\n",
    "    synthetic_weights, evals = generate_synthetic_weights(\n",
    "        real_weights, \n",
    "        GaussianMixture(n_components=n_comps, covariance_type='full')\n",
    "    )\n",
    "\n",
    "    for key in synthetic_weights:\n",
    "        if key not in gmm_synthetic_weights:\n",
    "            gmm_synthetic_weights[key] = {}\n",
    "    \n",
    "    for key in evals:\n",
    "        if key not in gmm_evals:\n",
    "            gmm_evals[key] = {}\n",
    "\n",
    "        gmm_synthetic_weights[key][n_comps] = synthetic_weights[key]\n",
    "        gmm_evals[key][n_comps] = evals[key]\n",
    "\n",
    "# Create and display the evaluation results DataFrame\n",
    "gmm_evals_df = pd.DataFrame(gmm_evals).reindex(range(1, 11))\n",

    "from sklearn.neighbors import KernelDensity\n",
    "\n",
    "bandwidths = np.logspace(0, -4, 15)\n",
    "kernels    = ['gaussian', 'tophat']\n",
    "kde_evals = {}\n",
    "kde_synthetic_weights = {}\n",
    "\n",
    "for bw in bandwidths:\n",
    "    for krnl in kernels:\n",
    "        synthetic_weights, evals = generate_synthetic_weights(\n",
    "            real_weights, \n",
    "            KernelDensity(kernel=krnl, bandwidth=bw)\n",
    "        )\n",
    "        \n",
    "        for key in synthetic_weights:\n",
    "            if key not in kde_synthetic_weights:\n",
    "                kde_synthetic_weights[key] = {}\n",
    "                \n",
    "        for key in evals:\n",
    "            if key not in kde_evals:\n",
    "                kde_evals[key] = {}\n",
    "\n",
    "            kde_synthetic_weights[key][(bw, krnl)] = synthetic_weights[key]\n",
    "            kde_evals[key][(bw, krnl)] = evals[key]\n",
    "\n",
    "# Create MultiIndex for (Kernel, Bandwidth) combinations\n",
    "rows = pd.MultiIndex.from_tuples(\n",
    "    [(krnl, f'bw={bw:.4f}') for bw in bandwidths for krnl in kernels],\n",
    "    names=['Kernel', 'Bandwidth']\n",
    ")\n",
    "\n",
    "# Build and sort DataFrame\n",
    "kde_eval_df = pd.DataFrame(kde_evals)\n",
    "kde_eval_df.index = rows\n",
    "kde_eval_df = kde_eval_df.sort_index()\n",
    "\n",
    "# Display the result\n",
    "kde_eval_df\n"
   ]

     ]
    }
   ],
   "source": [
    "# ============================================================\n",
    "# 0. REQUIRED INPUTS:\n",
    "#  - gmm_synthetic_weights\n",
    "#  - kde_synthetic_weights\n",
    "#  - source_matrices\n",
    "#  - scaling_remap\n",
    "#  - label_remap_config\n",
    "#  - colours_dict_label\n",
    "# ============================================================\n",
    "\n",
    "\n",
    "# ============================================================\n",
    "# 1. Relabel synthetic weights according to label_remap_config\n",
    "# ============================================================\n",
    "\n",
    "def relabel_weight_dfs(weight_dict, label_remap_config, method_name):\n",
    "    import copy\n",
    "    updated = copy.deepcopy(weight_dict)\n",
    "\n",
    "    for exp_key, param_dict in weight_dict.items():\n",
    "        remap = label_remap_config.get(exp_key, {}).get(method_name, {})\n",
    "\n",
    "        for param_key, df in param_dict.items():\n",
    "            df_copy = df.copy()\n",
    "\n",
    "            if remap:\n",
    "                print(f\"[Relabel] {exp_key} [{method_name}] param={param_key} using {remap}\")\n",
    "                df_copy[\"true_label\"] = df_copy[\"true_label\"].apply(lambda x: remap.get(x, x))\n",
    "\n",
    "            updated[exp_key][param_key] = df_copy\n",
    "\n",
    "    return updated\n",
    "\n",
    "\n",
    "gmm_weight_relabelled = relabel_weight_dfs(\n",
    "    gmm_synthetic_weights, label_remap_config, \"GMM\"\n",
    ")\n",
    "\n",
    "kde_weight_relabelled = relabel_weight_dfs(\n",
    "    kde_synthetic_weights, label_remap_config, \"KDE\"\n",
    ")\n",
    "\n",
    "\n",
    "\n",
    "# ============================================================\n",
    "# 2. Utility: FORCE NON-NEGATIVITY (weights or spectra)\n",
    "# ============================================================\n",
    "\n",
    "def force_non_negative(arr):\n",
    "    \"\"\"Clip negative values to zero.\"\"\"\n",
    "    return np.clip(arr, a_min=0, a_max=None)\n",
    "\n",
    "\n",
    "\n",
    "# ============================================================\n",
    "# 3. Reconstruct with constraints + scaling + relabelled weights\n",
    "# ============================================================\n",
    "\n",
    "reconstructed_df = {\n",
    "    \"GMM\": {},\n",
    "    \"KDE\": {}\n",
    "}\n",
    "\n",
    "synthetic_sources = {\n",
    "    \"GMM\": gmm_weight_relabelled,\n",
    "    \"KDE\": kde_weight_relabelled\n",
    "}\n",
    "\n",
    "\n",
    "for model_name, model_dict in synthetic_sources.items():\n",
    "\n",
    "    for key in model_dict.keys():\n",
    "\n",
    "        if key not in source_matrices:\n",
    "            continue\n",
    "\n",
    "        real_sources = source_matrices[key]       # shape: (2 or 3, N_points)\n",
    "        n_dim = real_sources.shape[0]\n",
    "\n",
    "        echo_type = \"LTE\" if \"LTE\" in key else \"STE\"\n",
    "\n",
    "        if key not in reconstructed_df[model_name]:\n",
    "            reconstructed_df[model_name][key] = {}\n",
    "\n",
    "        for param_key, df_syn in model_dict[key].items():\n",
    "\n",
    "            if param_key not in reconstructed_df[model_name][key]:\n",
    "                reconstructed_df[model_name][key][param_key] = []\n",
    "\n",
    "            df = df_syn.copy()\n",
    "\n",
    "            # ------------------------------------------------\n",
    "            # Select correct weight columns (2D or 3D)\n",
    "            # ------------------------------------------------\n",
    "            if n_dim == 2:\n",
    "                W = df[[\"w1\", \"w2\"]].to_numpy()\n",
    "            else:\n",
    "                W = df[[\"w1\", \"w2\", \"w3\"]].to_numpy()\n",
    "\n",
    "            labels = df[\"true_label\"].to_numpy()\n",
    "\n",
    "            # ------------------------------------------------\n",
    "            # FORCE NON-NEGATIVE WEIGHTS\n",
    "            # ------------------------------------------------\n",
    "            W = force_non_negative(W)\n",
    "\n",
    "            # ------------------------------------------------\n",
    "            # RECONSTRUCT (weights × sources)\n",
    "            # ------------------------------------------------\n",
    "            recon = np.dot(W, real_sources)\n",
    "\n",
    "            # ------------------------------------------------\n",
    "            # FORCE NON-NEGATIVE RECONSTRUCTION\n",
    "            # ------------------------------------------------\n",
    "            recon = force_non_negative(recon)\n",
    "\n",
    "            # ------------------------------------------------\n",
    "            # APPLY PER-LABEL scaling_remap\n",
    "            # ------------------------------------------------\n",
    "            recon_scaled = recon.copy()\n",
    "            unique_labels = np.unique(labels)\n",
    "\n",
    "            for lab in unique_labels:\n",
    "                mask = (labels == lab)\n",
    "                if not np.any(mask):\n",
    "                    continue\n",
    "\n",
    "                block = recon[mask]\n",
    "                label_max = block.max()\n",
    "\n",
    "                # avoid division by zero\n",
    "                if label_max <= 0 or np.isnan(label_max):\n",
    "                    scaling_factor = 1.0\n",
    "                else:\n",
    "                    target = scaling_remap.get(echo_type, {}).get(lab, 1.0)\n",
    "                    scaling_factor = target / label_max\n",
    "\n",
    "                    # add ±25% randomness (your original random*5 range was huge)\n",
    "                    variance = random.uniform(-0.25, 0.25)\n",
    "                    scaling_factor *= (1 + variance)\n",
    "\n",
    "                recon_scaled[mask] *= scaling_factor\n",
    "\n",
    "            # Final safety: no negative intensities\n",
    "            recon_scaled = force_non_negative(recon_scaled)\n",
    "\n",
    "            # ------------------------------------------------\n",
    "            # STORE\n",
    "            # ------------------------------------------------\n",
    "            reconstructed_df[model_name][key][param_key].append({\n",
    "                \"data\": recon_scaled,\n",
    "                \"labels\": labels\n",
    "            })\n",
    "\n",
    "\n",
    "# ============================================================\n",
    "# 4. Summary of what was created\n",
    "# ============================================================\n",
    "\n",
    "print(\"\\nSynthetic reconstructed spectra ready.\\n\")\n",
    "for model in reconstructed_df:\n",
    "    print(f\"MODEL {model}:\")\n",
    "    for key in reconstructed_df[model]:\n",
    "        print(f\"  {key}: params = {list(reconstructed_df[model][key].keys())}\")\n"
   ]
  },
  {

   "execution_count": 39,

    "    assert orig_len*2 == gmm_len == kde_len"

   "source": [
    "# ============================================================\n",
    "# PLOT SYNTHETIC WEIGHTS USING YOUR SD + KDE PLOTTING FUNCTION\n",
    "# ============================================================\n",
    "\n",
    "BEST_GMM_PARAM = 3\n",
    "BEST_KDE_PARAM = (0.001389495494373139, \"tophat\")\n",
    "\n",
    "\n",
    "def build_weight_df_from_synthetic(model_name, exp_key, best_param_key):\n",
    "    \"\"\"\n",
    "    Convert reconstructed_df[model][exp_key][param_key]\n",
    "    into a DataFrame identical to `real_weights[key]`.\n",
    "    \"\"\"\n",
    "    entry_list = reconstructed_df[model_name][exp_key][best_param_key]\n",
    "\n",
    "    # Usually the first entry is correct (your list only holds 1)\n",
    "    entry = entry_list[0]\n",
    "\n",
    "    syn_data = entry[\"data\"]      # ndarray, shape (N, n_points)\n",
    "    labels  = entry[\"labels\"]     # ndarray, shape (N,)\n",
    "\n",
    "    # But we need weights, not reconstructed spectra → so use synthetic WEIGHTS, not reconstructed spectra\n",
    "    # This is VERY important: reconstruct_df stores spectra, not weights\n",
    "    # So we use the *relabelled synthetic WEIGHT dictionary*\n",
    "    if model_name == \"GMM\":\n",
    "        syn_df = gmm_weight_relabelled[exp_key][best_param_key]\n",
    "    else:\n",
    "        syn_df = kde_weight_relabelled[exp_key][best_param_key]\n",
    "\n",
    "    # select only w1,w2,(w3) and true_label\n",
    "    cols = [c for c in syn_df.columns if c.startswith(\"w\")] + [\"true_label\"]\n",
    "    return syn_df[cols].copy()\n",
    "\n",
    "\n",
    "def plot_synthetic_for_experiment(exp_key):\n",
    "    \"\"\"\n",
    "    For a given experiment key (e.g. 'A2vNO@LTE'):\n",
    "        - Plot BEST GMM synthetic weights\n",
    "        - Plot BEST KDE synthetic weights\n",
    "    \"\"\"\n",
    "\n",
    "    print(\"\\n======================================================\")\n",
    "    print(f\"PLOTTING SYNTHETIC WEIGHTS FOR: {exp_key}\")\n",
    "    print(\"======================================================\\n\")\n",
    "\n",
    "    # ---------- GMM ----------\n",
    "    try:\n",
    "        syn_df_gmm = build_weight_df_from_synthetic(\"GMM\", exp_key, BEST_GMM_PARAM)\n",
    "        print(f\"[GMM] Plotting synthetic best-param={BEST_GMM_PARAM}\")\n",
    "        plot_weights_with_kde_and_outliers({exp_key: syn_df_gmm}, colours_dict_label)\n",
    "    except Exception as e:\n",
    "        print(f\"Skipping GMM for {exp_key}: {e}\")\n",
    "\n",
    "    # ---------- KDE ----------\n",
    "    try:\n",
    "        syn_df_kde = build_weight_df_from_synthetic(\"KDE\", exp_key, BEST_KDE_PARAM)\n",
    "        print(f\"[KDE] Plotting synthetic best-param={BEST_KDE_PARAM}\")\n",
    "        plot_weights_with_kde_and_outliers({exp_key: syn_df_kde}, colours_dict_label)\n",
    "    except Exception as e:\n",
    "        print(f\"Skipping KDE for {exp_key}: {e}\")\n",
    "\n",
    "\n",
    "# ============================================================\n",
    "# RUN FOR ALL EXPERIMENTS\n",
    "# ============================================================\n",
    "\n",
    "for exp_key in source_matrices.keys():\n",
    "    plot_synthetic_for_experiment(exp_key)\n"
   ]
  },

   "source": [
    "import numpy as np\n",
    "import pandas as pd\n",
    "import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n",
    "from scipy.stats import gaussian_kde, zscore\n",
    "from matplotlib import colors as mcolors, lines as mlines\n",
    "\n",
    "\n",
    "# ======================================================================\n",
    "# GLOBAL LIMITS\n",
    "# ======================================================================\n",
    "\n",
    "def compute_global_limits_for_model(\n",
    "    model,\n",
    "    raw_weights,\n",
    "    raw_data_frames,\n",
    "    gmm_weight_relabelled,\n",
    "    kde_weight_relabelled,\n",
    "    reconstructed_df,\n",
    "    best_gmm_param,\n",
    "    best_kde_param\n",
    "):\n",
    "    \"\"\"\n",
    "    Compute global axis limits across ALL experiments for a given model (\"GMM\" or \"KDE\"):\n",
    "      - w1, w2, w3 (from both real and other weights)\n",
    "      - raw spectra y-lims\n",
    "      - other spectra y-lims (reconstructed/synthetic)\n",
    "    \"\"\"\n",
    "\n",
    "    w1_min = np.inf; w1_max = -np.inf\n",
    "    w2_min = np.inf; w2_max = -np.inf\n",
    "    w3_min = np.inf; w3_max = -np.inf\n",
    "\n",
    "    raw_y_min = np.inf; raw_y_max = -np.inf\n",
    "    other_y_min = np.inf; other_y_max = -np.inf\n",
    "\n",
    "    for key in raw_weights.keys():\n",
    "        # ---------- REAL WEIGHTS ----------\n",
    "        rw = raw_weights[key]\n",
    "        if \"w1\" in rw.columns:\n",
    "            w1_min = min(w1_min, rw[\"w1\"].min())\n",
    "            w1_max = max(w1_max, rw[\"w1\"].max())\n",
    "        if \"w2\" in rw.columns:\n",
    "            w2_min = min(w2_min, rw[\"w2\"].min())\n",
    "            w2_max = max(w2_max, rw[\"w2\"].max())\n",
    "        if \"w3\" in rw.columns:\n",
    "            w3_min = min(w3_min, rw[\"w3\"].min())\n",
    "            w3_max = max(w3_max, rw[\"w3\"].max())\n",
    "\n",
    "        # ---------- OTHER WEIGHTS + OTHER SPECTRA ----------\n",
    "        if model == \"GMM\":\n",
    "            param_key = best_gmm_param\n",
    "            if key not in gmm_weight_relabelled:\n",
    "                continue\n",
    "            other_w = gmm_weight_relabelled[key][param_key]\n",
    "            entry = reconstructed_df[\"GMM\"][key][param_key][0]\n",
    "        else:\n",
    "            param_key = best_kde_param\n",
    "            if key not in kde_weight_relabelled:\n",
    "                continue\n",
    "            other_w = kde_weight_relabelled[key][param_key]\n",
    "            entry = reconstructed_df[\"KDE\"][key][param_key][0]\n",
    "\n",
    "        if \"w1\" in other_w.columns:\n",
    "            w1_min = min(w1_min, other_w[\"w1\"].min())\n",
    "            w1_max = max(w1_max, other_w[\"w1\"].max())\n",
    "        if \"w2\" in other_w.columns:\n",
    "            w2_min = min(w2_min, other_w[\"w2\"].min())\n",
    "            w2_max = max(w2_max, other_w[\"w2\"].max())\n",
    "        if \"w3\" in other_w.columns:\n",
    "            w3_min = min(w3_min, other_w[\"w3\"].min())\n",
    "            w3_max = max(w3_max, other_w[\"w3\"].max())\n",
    "\n",
    "        # ---------- RAW SPECTRA ----------\n",
    "        raw_df = raw_data_frames[key]\n",
    "        spec_cols = [c for c in raw_df.columns if c != \"labels\"]\n",
    "        raw_y_min = min(raw_y_min, raw_df[spec_cols].min().min())\n",
    "        raw_y_max = max(raw_y_max, raw_df[spec_cols].max().max())\n",
    "\n",
    "        # ---------- OTHER SPECTRA ----------\n",
    "        other_data = entry[\"data\"]  # ndarray\n",
    "        other_y_min = min(other_y_min, np.min(other_data))\n",
    "        other_y_max = max(other_y_max, np.max(other_data))\n",
    "\n",
    "    # small buffers\n",
    "    if np.isfinite(w1_min) and np.isfinite(w1_max):\n",
    "        w1_range = w1_max - w1_min\n",
    "        w1_min -= 0.05 * w1_range\n",
    "        w1_max += 0.05 * w1_range\n",
    "    if np.isfinite(w2_min) and np.isfinite(w2_max):\n",
    "        w2_range = w2_max - w2_min\n",
    "        w2_min -= 0.05 * w2_range\n",
    "        w2_max += 0.05 * w2_range\n",
    "    if np.isfinite(w3_min) and np.isfinite(w3_max):\n",
    "        w3_range = w3_max - w3_min\n",
    "        w3_min -= 0.05 * w3_range\n",
    "        w3_max += 0.05 * w3_range\n",
    "\n",
    "    return {\n",
    "        \"w1\": (w1_min, w1_max),\n",
    "        \"w2\": (w2_min, w2_max),\n",
    "        \"w3\": (w3_min if np.isfinite(w3_min) else None,\n",
    "               w3_max if np.isfinite(w3_max) else None),\n",
    "        \"raw_y\": (raw_y_min, raw_y_max),\n",
    "        \"other_y\": (other_y_min, other_y_max),\n",
    "    }\n",
    "\n",
    "\n",
    "# ======================================================================\n",
    "# SHADING\n",
    "# ======================================================================\n",
    "\n",
    "def compute_shading(weights_df, colours_dict_label):\n",
    "    \"\"\"Assigns consistent shading for all plots (used in scatter + spectra).\"\"\"\n",
    "    w_cols = [c for c in weights_df.columns if c.startswith(\"w\")]\n",
    "    d = np.linalg.norm(weights_df[w_cols].values, axis=1)\n",
    "    dmin, dmax = d.min(), d.max()\n",
    "\n",
    "    shades = [\n",
    "        shade_by_distance(colours_dict_label.get(lbl, \"black\"), dist, dmin, dmax)\n",
    "        for lbl, dist in zip(weights_df[\"true_label\"], d)\n",
    "    ]\n",
    "    weights_df[\"shade\"] = shades\n",
    "    return weights_df\n",
    "\n",
    "\n",
    "# ======================================================================\n",
    "# SCATTER HELPERS\n",
    "# ======================================================================\n",
    "\n",
    "def plot_simple_scatter(ax, df, colours_dict_label, global_limits):\n",
    "    \"\"\"Simple 2D/3D scatter for OTHER weights (no SD, no outliers).\"\"\"\n",
    "    is_3d = (\"w3\" in df.columns)\n",
    "\n",
    "    if not is_3d:\n",
    "        for lab in sorted(df[\"true_label\"].unique()):\n",
    "            sub = df[df[\"true_label\"] == lab]\n",
    "            ax.scatter(\n",
    "                sub[\"w1\"], sub[\"w2\"],\n",
    "                c=list(sub[\"shade\"]),\n",
    "                s=45, edgecolor=\"k\", linewidth=0.3,\n",
    "                label=lab\n",
    "            )\n",
    "        ax.set_xlabel(\"w1\")\n",
    "        ax.set_ylabel(\"w2\")\n",
    "        ax.set_xlim(*global_limits[\"w1\"])\n",
    "        ax.set_ylim(*global_limits[\"w2\"])\n",
    "        ax.legend(title=\"Label\")\n",
    "    else:\n",
    "        for lab in sorted(df[\"true_label\"].unique()):\n",
    "            sub = df[df[\"true_label\"] == lab]\n",
    "            ax.scatter(\n",
    "                sub[\"w1\"], sub[\"w2\"], sub[\"w3\"],\n",
    "                c=list(sub[\"shade\"]),\n",
    "                s=45, edgecolor=\"k\", linewidth=0.3,\n",
    "                label=lab\n",
    "            )\n",
    "        ax.set_xlabel(\"w1\")\n",
    "        ax.set_ylabel(\"w2\")\n",
    "        ax.set_zlabel(\"w3\")\n",
    "        ax.set_xlim(*global_limits[\"w1\"])\n",
    "        ax.set_ylim(*global_limits[\"w2\"])\n",
    "        if global_limits[\"w3\"][0] is not None:\n",
    "            ax.set_zlim(*global_limits[\"w3\"])\n",
    "        ax.legend(title=\"Label\")\n",
    "\n",
    "\n",
    "def plot_gaussian_ellipsoid(ax, mean, cov, n_std=1.0, color=\"C0\", alpha=0.15, n_points=40):\n",
    "    vals, vecs = np.linalg.eigh(cov + 1e-8 * np.eye(3))\n",
    "    radii = n_std * np.sqrt(np.maximum(vals, 0))\n",
    "\n",
    "    u = np.linspace(0, 2*np.pi, n_points)\n",
    "    v = np.linspace(0, np.pi, n_points)\n",
    "\n",
    "    x = np.outer(np.cos(u), np.sin(v))\n",
    "    y = np.outer(np.sin(u), np.sin(v))\n",
    "    z = np.outer(np.ones_like(u), np.cos(v))\n",
    "\n",
    "    xyz = np.stack((x, y, z), axis=-1)\n",
    "    xyz *= radii\n",
    "    xyz = xyz @ vecs.T\n",
    "    xyz += mean\n",
    "\n",
    "    X, Y, Z = xyz[..., 0], xyz[..., 1], xyz[..., 2]\n",
    "    ax.plot_surface(\n",
    "        X, Y, Z,\n",
    "        rstride=1, cstride=1,\n",
    "        color=color, alpha=alpha,\n",
    "        linewidth=0, shade=True\n",
    "    )\n",
    "\n",
    "\n",
    "def plot_advanced_scatter(ax, df, colours_dict_label, global_limits, outlier_sd=3.0, gridsize=120, use_kde_countours=False):\n",
    "    \"\"\"\n",
    "    Advanced scatter:\n",
    "      - 2D: Gaussian SD rings (1/2/3σ), KDE contours, z-score outliers.\n",
    "      - 3D: Gaussian ellipsoids (1/2/3σ) + z-score outliers.\n",
    "    \"\"\"\n",
    "    is_3d = (\"w3\" in df.columns)\n",
    "\n",
    "    # --------------------------------------------------------------\n",
    "    # 2D\n",
    "    # --------------------------------------------------------------\n",
    "    if not is_3d:\n",
    "        xlims = global_limits[\"w1\"]\n",
    "        ylims = global_limits[\"w2\"]\n",
    "\n",
    "        xg = np.linspace(*xlims, gridsize)\n",
    "        yg = np.linspace(*ylims, gridsize)\n",
    "        X, Y = np.meshgrid(xg, yg)\n",
    "        pts = np.column_stack([X.ravel(), Y.ravel()])\n",
    "\n",
    "        for lab in sorted(df[\"true_label\"].unique()):\n",
    "            sub = df[df[\"true_label\"] == lab].copy()\n",
    "            if sub.empty:\n",
    "                continue\n",
    "\n",
    "            base_color = colours_dict_label.get(lab, \"C0\")\n",
    "            data = sub[[\"w1\", \"w2\"]].astype(float).to_numpy()\n",
    "\n",
    "            # KDE contours\n",
    "            if len(sub) > 3:\n",
    "                if use_kde_countours:\n",
    "                    try:\n",
    "                        kde = gaussian_kde(data.T)\n",
    "                        Z = kde(pts.T).reshape(X.shape)\n",
    "                        ax.contour(X, Y, Z, levels=5, colors=[base_color], alpha=0.5)\n",
    "                    except Exception:\n",
    "                        pass\n",
    "\n",
    "                # Gaussian SD rings\n",
    "                mean = data.mean(axis=0)\n",
    "                cov = np.cov(data, rowvar=False) + np.eye(2) * 1e-6\n",
    "                cov_inv = np.linalg.pinv(cov)\n",
    "\n",
    "                diff = pts - mean\n",
    "                d2 = np.einsum(\"ij,jk,ik->i\", diff, cov_inv, diff).reshape(X.shape)\n",
    "                D = np.sqrt(d2)\n",
    "\n",
    "                alphas = [0.45, 0.25, 0.12]\n",
    "                for i in range(3):\n",
    "                    col = np.array(mcolors.to_rgba(base_color))\n",
    "                    col[3] = alphas[i]\n",
    "                    ax.contourf(\n",
    "                        X, Y, D,\n",
    "                        levels=[i, i + 1],\n",
    "                        colors=[col],\n",
    "                        antialiased=True\n",
    "                    )\n",
    "\n",
    "                ax.contour(X, Y, D, levels=[1, 2, 3],\n",
    "                           colors=[base_color], linewidths=1.2)\n",
    "\n",
    "            ax.scatter(\n",
    "                sub[\"w1\"], sub[\"w2\"],\n",
    "                c=list(sub[\"shade\"]),\n",
    "                s=45, edgecolor=\"k\", linewidth=0.3\n",
    "            )\n",
    "\n",
    "        # Outliers\n",
    "        all_out = []\n",
    "        for lab in sorted(df[\"true_label\"].unique()):\n",
    "            sub = df[df[\"true_label\"] == lab].copy()\n",
    "            if sub.empty:\n",
    "                continue\n",
    "            zs = np.abs(sub[[\"w1\", \"w2\"]].apply(zscore))\n",
    "            out_mask = (zs > outlier_sd).any(axis=1)\n",
    "            for _, row in sub.loc[out_mask].iterrows():\n",
    "                all_out.append((lab, row[\"w1\"], row[\"w2\"]))\n",
    "                ax.scatter(\n",
    "                    row[\"w1\"], row[\"w2\"],\n",
    "                    s=160, facecolors=\"none\",\n",
    "                    edgecolors=\"red\", linewidths=1.8\n",
    "                )\n",
    "\n",
    "        ax.set_xlim(*xlims)\n",
    "        ax.set_ylim(*ylims)\n",
    "        ax.set_xlabel(\"w1\")\n",
    "        ax.set_ylabel(\"w2\")\n",
    "\n",
    "        # Legend\n",
    "        legend_handles = []\n",
    "        legend_labels  = []\n",
    "\n",
    "        # 1) class handles\n",
    "        for lab in sorted(df[\"true_label\"].unique()):\n",
    "            legend_handles.append(\n",
    "                mlines.Line2D([], [], marker=\"o\",\n",
    "                            color=colours_dict_label.get(lab,\"C0\"),\n",
    "                            markersize=8, linestyle=\"None\")\n",
    "            )\n",
    "            legend_labels.append(lab)\n",
    "\n",
    "        # 2) outlier entry (with count)\n",
    "        out_count = len(all_out)\n",
    "        legend_handles.append(\n",
    "            mlines.Line2D([], [], marker=\"o\", color=\"none\",\n",
    "                        markerfacecolor=\"none\", markeredgecolor=\"red\",\n",
    "                        markersize=10)\n",
    "        )\n",
    "        legend_labels.append(f\"Outliers (n={out_count})\")\n",
    "\n",
    "        ax.legend(legend_handles, legend_labels, title=\"Label\", loc=\"upper left\")\n",
    "\n",
    "\n",
    "    # --------------------------------------------------------------\n",
    "    # 3D\n",
    "    # --------------------------------------------------------------\n",
    "    else:\n",
    "        xlims = global_limits[\"w1\"]\n",
    "        ylims = global_limits[\"w2\"]\n",
    "        zlims = global_limits[\"w3\"]\n",
    "\n",
    "        all_out = []\n",
    "        for lab in sorted(df[\"true_label\"].unique()):\n",
    "            sub = df[df[\"true_label\"] == lab].copy()\n",
    "            if sub.empty:\n",
    "                continue\n",
    "\n",
    "            base_color = colours_dict_label.get(lab, \"C0\")\n",
    "\n",
    "            zs = np.abs(sub[[\"w1\", \"w2\", \"w3\"]].apply(zscore))\n",
    "            out_mask = (zs > outlier_sd).any(axis=1)\n",
    "            sub[\"is_outlier\"] = out_mask\n",
    "\n",
    "            inliers = sub.loc[~out_mask, [\"w1\", \"w2\", \"w3\"]].astype(float).to_numpy()\n",
    "            if len(inliers) >= 5:\n",
    "                mean3 = inliers.mean(axis=0)\n",
    "                cov3 = np.cov(inliers, rowvar=False)\n",
    "                for n_std, alpha in zip([1, 2, 3], [0.30, 0.18, 0.10]):\n",
    "                    plot_gaussian_ellipsoid(ax, mean3, cov3, n_std=n_std,\n",
    "                                            color=base_color, alpha=alpha)\n",
    "\n",
    "            ax.scatter(\n",
    "                sub.loc[~out_mask, \"w1\"],\n",
    "                sub.loc[~out_mask, \"w2\"],\n",
    "                sub.loc[~out_mask, \"w3\"],\n",
    "                c=list(sub.loc[~out_mask, \"shade\"]),\n",
    "                s=40, label=lab, zorder=5\n",
    "            )\n",
    "\n",
    "            for _, row in sub.loc[out_mask].iterrows():\n",
    "                all_out.append((lab, row[\"w1\"], row[\"w2\"], row[\"w3\"]))\n",
    "                ax.scatter(\n",
    "                    row[\"w1\"], row[\"w2\"], row[\"w3\"],\n",
    "                    s=300, facecolors=\"none\", edgecolors=\"red\",\n",
    "                    linewidths=2, zorder=3\n",
    "                )\n",
    "\n",
    "        ax.set_xlim(*xlims)\n",
    "        ax.set_ylim(*ylims)\n",
    "        if zlims[0] is not None:\n",
    "            ax.set_zlim(*zlims)\n",
    "        ax.set_xlabel(\"w1\")\n",
    "        ax.set_ylabel(\"w2\")\n",
    "        ax.set_zlabel(\"w3\")\n",
    "\n",
    "        handles, labels = ax.get_legend_handles_labels()\n",
    "        uniq = {}\n",
    "        for h, l in zip(handles, labels):\n",
    "            if l not in uniq:\n",
    "                uniq[l] = h\n",
    "        handles = list(uniq.values())\n",
    "        labels = list(uniq.keys())\n",
    "        out_h = mlines.Line2D([], [], marker=\"o\", color=\"none\",\n",
    "                              markerfacecolor=\"none\", markeredgecolor=\"red\",\n",
    "                              markersize=10)\n",
    "        handles.append(out_h)\n",
    "        labels.append(f\"Outliers (n={len(all_out)})\")\n",
    "        ax.legend(handles, labels, title=\"Label\", loc=\"upper left\")\n",
    "\n",
    "\n",
    "# ======================================================================\n",
    "# FULL PAGE: 2 ROWS × (1 + N_CLASSES) COLUMNS\n",
    "# ======================================================================\n",
    "def plot_full_page(\n",
    "    real_weights_df,\n",
    "    raw_df,\n",
    "    other_weights_df,\n",
    "    other_spectra_data,\n",
    "    other_labels,\n",
    "    colours_dict_label,\n",
    "    global_limits,\n",
    "    outlier_sd=3.0,\n",
    "    gridsize=120,\n",
    "    use_kde_countours=False\n",
    "):\n",
    "    \"\"\"\n",
    "    Layout for n classes:\n",
    "\n",
    "      ROW 0: [ advanced scatter (REAL weights) | RAW spectra per class ]\n",
    "      ROW 1: [ simple scatter (OTHER weights)  | OTHER spectra per class ]\n",
    "    \"\"\"\n",
    "\n",
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n",
    "    # FIX 3 — ensure remapping happens BEFORE shading\n",
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n",
    "    real_weights_df = real_weights_df.copy()\n",
    "    other_weights_df = other_weights_df.copy()\n",
    "\n",
    "    if \"true_label\" not in real_weights_df.columns and \"label\" in real_weights_df.columns:\n",
    "        real_weights_df[\"true_label\"] = real_weights_df[\"label\"]\n",
    "    if \"true_label\" not in other_weights_df.columns and \"label\" in other_weights_df.columns:\n",
    "        other_weights_df[\"true_label\"] = other_weights_df[\"label\"]\n",
    "\n",
    "    # force string labels BEFORE shading\n",
    "    real_weights_df[\"true_label\"] = real_weights_df[\"true_label\"].astype(str)\n",
    "    other_weights_df[\"true_label\"] = other_weights_df[\"true_label\"].astype(str)\n",
    "\n",
    "    # shading AFTER correct labels\n",
    "    real_weights_df = compute_shading(real_weights_df, colours_dict_label)\n",
    "    other_weights_df = compute_shading(other_weights_df, colours_dict_label)\n",
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n",
    "\n",
    "\n",
    "    unique_labels = sorted(real_weights_df[\"true_label\"].unique())\n",
    "    n_classes = len(unique_labels)\n",
    "\n",
    "    # Build OTHER spectra DataFrame\n",
    "    spec_cols = [c for c in raw_df.columns if c != \"labels\"]\n",
    "    other_spectra_df = pd.DataFrame(other_spectra_data, columns=spec_cols)\n",
    "    other_spectra_df[\"labels\"] = other_labels\n",
    "\n",
    "    fig = plt.figure(figsize=(10 + 5*n_classes, 6 + 4*n_classes), dpi=500)\n",
    "    gs = fig.add_gridspec(\n",
    "        nrows=2,\n",
    "        ncols=1 + n_classes,\n",
    "        width_ratios=[2.5] + [2]*n_classes,\n",
    "        height_ratios=[1, 1],\n",
    "        hspace=0.28,\n",
    "        wspace=0.25,\n",
    "    )\n",
    "\n",
    "    # ---------------- ROW 0, COL 0: ADVANCED REAL SCATTER ----------------\n",
    "    ax0 = fig.add_subplot(gs[0, 0], projection=\"3d\" if \"w3\" in real_weights_df.columns else None)\n",
    "    plot_advanced_scatter(\n",
    "        ax0,\n",
    "        real_weights_df,\n",
    "        colours_dict_label,\n",
    "        global_limits,\n",
    "        outlier_sd=outlier_sd,\n",
    "        gridsize=gridsize,\n",
    "        use_kde_countours=use_kde_countours\n",
    "    )\n",
    "    ax0.set_title(\"REAL weights: SD rings/ellipsoids + outliers + KDE\")\n",
    "\n",
    "    # ---------------- ROW 0, COL 1..N: RAW SPECTRA PER CLASS -------------\n",
    "    raw_ymin, raw_ymax = global_limits[\"raw_y\"]\n",
    "    other_ymin, other_ymax = global_limits[\"other_y\"]\n",
    "    spec_cols = [c for c in raw_df.columns if c != \"labels\"]\n",
    "    x = np.arange(len(spec_cols))\n",
    "\n",
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n",
    "    # FIX 2 for shared Y-axis between top and bottom row:\n",
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n",
    "    combined_ymin = min(raw_ymin, other_ymin)\n",
    "    combined_ymax = max(raw_ymax, other_ymax)\n",
    "    ybuf = 0.05 * (combined_ymax - combined_ymin)\n",
    "    combined_ymin -= ybuf\n",
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n",
    "\n",
    "    for i, lab in enumerate(unique_labels):\n",
    "        ax = fig.add_subplot(gs[0, 1 + i])\n",
    "        sub_raw = raw_df[raw_df[\"labels\"] == lab]\n",
    "\n",
    "        for idx in sub_raw.index:\n",
    "            if idx < len(real_weights_df):\n",
    "                col = real_weights_df[\"shade\"].iloc[idx]\n",
    "            else:\n",
    "                col = colours_dict_label.get(lab, \"k\")\n",
    "            ax.plot(x, sub_raw.loc[idx, spec_cols].values, color=col, alpha=0.8)\n",
    "\n",
    "        ax.set_title(f\"Raw – {lab}\")\n",
    "        ax.set_xlabel(\"Index\")\n",
    "        ax.set_ylabel(\"Intensity\")\n",
    "        ax.set_ylim(combined_ymin, combined_ymax)   ### FIX 2\n",
    "        ax.grid(True)\n",
    "\n",
    "    # ---------------- ROW 1, COL 0: SIMPLE OTHER SCATTER -----------------\n",
    "    ax1 = fig.add_subplot(gs[1, 0], projection=\"3d\" if \"w3\" in other_weights_df.columns else None)\n",
    "    plot_simple_scatter(ax1, other_weights_df, colours_dict_label, global_limits)\n",
    "    ax1.set_title(\"OTHER weights (synthetic/reconstructed): simple scatter\")\n",
    "\n",
    "    # ---------------- ROW 1, COL 1..N: OTHER SPECTRA PER CLASS ----------\n",
    "    x2 = np.arange(len(spec_cols))\n",
    "\n",
    "    for i, lab in enumerate(unique_labels):\n",
    "        ax = fig.add_subplot(gs[1, 1 + i])\n",
    "        sub_other = other_spectra_df[other_spectra_df[\"labels\"] == lab]\n",
    "\n",
    "        for idx in sub_other.index:\n",
    "            if idx < len(other_weights_df):\n",
    "                col = other_weights_df[\"shade\"].iloc[idx]\n",
    "            else:\n",
    "                col = colours_dict_label.get(lab, \"k\")\n",
    "            ax.plot(x2, sub_other.loc[idx, spec_cols].values, color=col, alpha=0.8)\n",
    "\n",
    "        ax.set_title(f\"Other – {lab}\")\n",
    "        ax.set_xlabel(\"Index\")\n",
    "        ax.set_ylabel(\"Intensity\")\n",
    "        ax.set_ylim(combined_ymin, combined_ymax)   ### FIX 2\n",
    "        ax.grid(True)\n",
    "\n",
    "    plt.tight_layout()\n",
    "    plt.close(fig)\n",
    "    #plt.show()\n",
    "\n",
    "\n",
    "\n",
    "# ======================================================================\n",
    "# EXAMPLE USAGE LOOP (ADAPT AS NEEDED)\n",
    "# ======================================================================\n",
    "\n",
    "BEST_GMM_PARAM = 3\n",
    "BEST_KDE_PARAM = (0.001389495494373139, \"tophat\")\n",
    "\n",
    "for model in [\"GMM\", \"KDE\"]:\n",
    "    global_limits = compute_global_limits_for_model(\n",
    "        model=model,\n",
    "        raw_weights=raw_weights,                 # dict: key -> real weights df\n",
    "        raw_data_frames=raw_data_frames,         # dict: key -> raw spectra df\n",
    "        gmm_weight_relabelled=gmm_weight_relabelled,\n",
    "        kde_weight_relabelled=kde_weight_relabelled,\n",
    "        reconstructed_df=reconstructed_df,       # nested dict built earlier\n",
    "        best_gmm_param=BEST_GMM_PARAM,\n",
    "        best_kde_param=BEST_KDE_PARAM\n",
    "    )\n",
    "\n",
    "    for key in raw_weights.keys():\n",
    "        # remove this if-block to plot all experiments\n",
    "        # if key != \"MMvME@STE\":\n",
    "        #     continue\n",
    "\n",
    "        if model == \"GMM\":\n",
    "            param_key = BEST_GMM_PARAM\n",
    "            other_weights_df = gmm_weight_relabelled[key][param_key]\n",
    "            syn_entry = reconstructed_df[\"GMM\"][key][param_key][0]\n",
    "        else:\n",
    "            param_key = BEST_KDE_PARAM\n",
    "            other_weights_df = kde_weight_relabelled[key][param_key]\n",
    "            syn_entry = reconstructed_df[\"KDE\"][key][param_key][0]\n",
    "\n",
    "        other_spectra_data = syn_entry[\"data\"]\n",
    "        other_labels = syn_entry[\"labels\"]\n",
    "\n",
    "        plot_full_page(\n",
    "            real_weights_df=raw_weights[key].copy(),\n",
    "            raw_df=raw_data_frames[key],\n",
    "            other_weights_df=other_weights_df,\n",
    "            other_spectra_data=other_spectra_data,\n",
    "            other_labels=other_labels,\n",
    "            colours_dict_label=colours_dict_label,\n",
    "            global_limits=global_limits,\n",
    "            outlier_sd=3.0,\n",
    "            gridsize=150\n",
    "        )\n"
   ]
  },
